- **sec-llm-auditor**: Agente autônomo de auditoria de segurança, privacidade (LGPD/PII) e qualidade de arquitetura para PRs e esteiras de CI/CD. ([robertosrjr/consignado](https://github.com/robertosrjr/consignado/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/sec-llm-auditor.md) · 2 copies)
- **sec-logic**: Business logic, TOCTOU race condition, atomic database transaction, financial/payment/coupon integrity, and multi-step workflow state-machine analyst (CWE-362, CWE-367, CWE-840, CWE-841). ([webdotpulse/Antigravity-security](https://github.com/webdotpulse/Antigravity-security/blob/HEAD/agents/sec-logic.md))
- **sec-map**: SAST フェーズ1a 攻撃面マップ専任。Source / Sink / Trust Boundary を全件抽出して ATTACK_SURFACE.md に保存する。オーケストレータ (/sec-run) から起動される。 ([shinya-shibasaki/security-audit-workspace](https://github.com/shinya-shibasaki/security-audit-workspace/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/sec-map.md))
- **sec-offense**: Red team adversarial security tester. Use for offensive security testing, writing exploit proof-of-concepts, probing defenses, testing bypass attempts, or validating that security modules actually block attacks. Requires clear authorization context. Triggers on: red team, penetration test, exploit, attack simulation, bypass testing, adversarial probe, security validation. ([idallasjlabs/agentshroud](https://github.com/idallasjlabs/agentshroud/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/sec-offense.md) · 4 copies)
- **sec-ops**: 1. **Autoridad del Dominio vs. Contexto de Sesión:** Las reglas de negocio, el modelo de datos y la nomenclatura están dictados ÚNICAMENTE por los artefactos normativos vigentes y aprobados. El contexto de la sesión actual determina *qué tarea* se ejecuta, pero ninguna instrucción informal de la sesión puede invalidar las reglas de gobernanza aprobadas. 2. **Resolución de Conflictos Normativos:** ([Gera11ChR/P2-warehouse-stock](https://github.com/Gera11ChR/P2-warehouse-stock/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/sec-ops.md))
- **sec-ops-expert**: Security and operations auditor. Reviews diffs and live cluster state for OWASP issues, secrets exposure, RBAC posture, container hardening, supply-chain risks. Read-only edit; read-only kubectl allowed. Outputs CRITICAL/HIGH/MEDIUM/LOW findings. ([CleveritDemo/servalai-cli](https://github.com/CleveritDemo/servalai-cli/blob/HEAD/assets/default-bundle/agents/sec-ops-expert.md) · 2 copies)
- **sec-orchestrator**: Coordinates multi-agent ClaudeSec security workflows — triages scan findings by severity, assigns work to sec-researcher / sec-implementer / sec-reviewer, tracks progress, and accepts completion. Use when a security task spans multiple steps or agents (full scan + remediation, security-guide authoring, scanner-feature delivery, hotfix). Delegates rather than implementing directly. ([Twodragon0/claudesec](https://github.com/Twodragon0/claudesec/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/sec-orchestrator.md) · ★15)
- **sec-orchestrator**: Lead application-security research orchestrator. Use to run a full vulnerability hunt on a cloned/local source target, a live website authorized by a bug-bounty scope file, or both (grey-box): verifies scope, plans the hunt, runs the static+dynamic pipeline, routes leads to specialist hunters, enforces responsible-disclosure guardrails, and produces a submittable report. Invoked by the /hunt command. Prefer continuing an existing orchestrator via SendMessage over spawning a new one. ([YashvantHange/Orc](https://github.com/YashvantHange/Orc/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/sec-orchestrator.md) · ★1)
- **sec-orchestrator**: Entry-point AppSec router that standardizes intake, delegates to specialist agents, and synthesizes evidence-first outputs. ([viniciusciconebarbosa/antigravity-cli-security-framework](https://github.com/viniciusciconebarbosa/antigravity-cli-security-framework/blob/HEAD/agents/sec-orchestrator.agent.md))
- **sec-patcher**: Security-mode subagent, optional. Opus (`cliproxy/claude-opus-5-5`), then GLM. Patches the source of bad state in a target worktree on the hunt device, then reruns PoV and tests. Never pushes. Full shipped prompt: `packages/core/src/agents-defaults/sec-patcher.md`. ([default-cybe/conclave](https://github.com/default-cybe/conclave/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/sec-patcher.md) · ★3)
- **sec-privacy-engineering**: Design privacy-protecting systems — GDPR / CCPA / HIPAA compliance, data minimisation, anonymisation, consent management, data retention, breach notification — with explicit privacy impact assessme... ([quantumquirkxyz/skills-quirk](https://github.com/quantumquirkxyz/skills-quirk/blob/HEAD/.agents/skills/platform/adapters/claude/agents/sec-sec-privacy-engineering.md))
- **sec-recon**: Security recon persona: the BREADTH half of the mandatory two-pass audit. High-recall sweep that walks the property×surface checklist across every file in scope. Optimized for exhaustive coverage and surface enumeration, NOT for adjudication: every candidate it emits is later refuted by sec-auditor. Spawned by the adversarial-tests pass. Read .claude/skills/adversarial-tests/SKILL.md first. ([DonaldMurillo/gofastr](https://github.com/DonaldMurillo/gofastr/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/sec-recon.md) · ★24)
- **sec-recon**: Security-mode subagent. GLM (`zai/glm-5.3`), then Opus. Reads code, scope, catalogue, parked leads, proxy history and advisories; writes `targets/&lt;t&gt;/brief.md`. No new target traffic. Uses semgrep, Caido and REA MCP, bughunt-catalogue and audit-context-building. Full shipped prompt: `packages/core/src/agents-defaults/sec-recon.md`. ([default-cybe/conclave](https://github.com/default-cybe/conclave/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/sec-recon.md) · ★3)
- **sec-recon**: Reconnaissance & attack-surface cartographer. Runs detect_stack.py and scan_sinks.py to inventory architecture, entry points, authentication boundaries, data flows, and candidate sinks into deliverables/code_analysis_deliverable.md. ([webdotpulse/Antigravity-security](https://github.com/webdotpulse/Antigravity-security/blob/HEAD/agents/sec-recon.md))
- **sec-redteam**: &gt; Brain v1.0 · 2026-11-06 · written by the Brain Creator (Open work A). Tier: `worker`. Security team &gt; (law 25). Defensive only: you prove our own holes in our own sandbox. ([jamilabs-solutions/Starfish](https://github.com/jamilabs-solutions/Starfish/blob/HEAD/agents/sec-redteam.md))
- **sec-refuter**: Security-mode subagent. Daybreak (`cliproxy/personal-gpt/gpt-daybreak-blue-latest`), then GLM. Reads lead and code, checks lowest-role reachability, documented boundary, downstream validator. Returns tested and untested assumptions with commands; no live traffic. Full shipped prompt: `packages/core/src/agents-defaults/sec-refuter.md`. ([default-cybe/conclave](https://github.com/default-cybe/conclave/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/sec-refuter.md) · ★3)
- **sec-report**: SAST フェーズ8 レポート化専任。フェーズ7 で検証済みの findings(curl 結果と再現手順を含む)を開発者向けに翻訳し reports/&lt;finding-id&gt;.md として保存する。dedupe を最初に行い、dismissed は除外、verified を優先。オーケストレータが起動する。 ([shinya-shibasaki/security-audit-workspace](https://github.com/shinya-shibasaki/security-audit-workspace/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/sec-report.md))
- **sec-reporter**: Security-mode subagent. Opus (`cliproxy/claude-opus-5-5`), then GLM. Reads validator's confirmed evidence, writes a first-person `REPORT.md` in findings, selects venue, never submits. Skill: avoid-ai-writing. Full shipped prompt: `packages/core/src/agents-defaults/sec-reporter.md`. ([default-cybe/conclave](https://github.com/default-cybe/conclave/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/sec-reporter.md) · ★3)
- **sec-repro**: SAST フェーズ7 再現確認専任。各 finding に対し (a) curl で実行可能なものは自走で再現実行し verified/dismissed/inconclusive に分類、(b) curl の成否に関わらずブラウザ + DevTools の人間向け手順を生成。結果は findings に追記する(レポート化はフェーズ8)。curl の宛先は localhost 系のみ(フックで強制)。 ([shinya-shibasaki/security-audit-workspace](https://github.com/shinya-shibasaki/security-audit-workspace/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/sec-repro.md))
- **sec-researcher**: Read-only security research for ClaudeSec — threats, CVEs, attack vectors, and compliance frameworks (NIST, ISO 27001, ISMS-P). Use when a task needs authoritative evidence (OWASP / NIST / CIS / MITRE ATLAS) before any code or docs change. Gathers and cites; does not modify files. ([Twodragon0/claudesec](https://github.com/Twodragon0/claudesec/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/sec-researcher.md) · ★15)
- **sec-review**: Barrido de seguridad sobre un área completa — un repo, una configuración, una superficie expuesta — buscando secretos versionados, permisos demasiado amplios, exposición pública no intencionada y dependencias con vulnerabilidad conocida. Use to triage high/critical findings before they go in the report, and to filter scanner false positives. - **SECGEN_ADAPTABLE_CATALOGUE**: These SecGen scenarios are live and known-good. A mission's VM **may be rebased on one of these** if it enhances play (user, this pass). Treat a rebase as a design improvement, not a mere consistency fix: it means adapting the chosen scenario's systems/modules into the mission's canonical SecGen XML (`break_escape/safetynet/&lt;mission&gt;.xml`) and re-aligning every game reference (flags, accounts, hos ([cliffe/BreakEscape](https://github.com/cliffe/BreakEscape/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/SECGEN_ADAPTABLE_CATALOGUE.md) · ★6)
- **SECGEN_CLAIMS_CHECK**: Fact check of the game text against the SecGen scenario XML and module metadata. No exploit detail is recorded here. ([cliffe/BreakEscape](https://github.com/cliffe/BreakEscape/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/SECGEN_CLAIMS_CHECK.md) · ★6)
- **SECGEN_MODULE_PLAN**: Written for the author. Goal: stop over-using distcc (currently m03, m04, m06), draw every substitute from the **known-good catalogue scenarios** (so they are Bookworm-tested — shellshock is not), and line up VM module candidates for the unauthored missions (m09, m10). ([cliffe/BreakEscape](https://github.com/cliffe/BreakEscape/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/SECGEN_MODULE_PLAN.md) · ★6)
- **SECGEN_REVIEW_BRIEF**: You are reviewing and fixing one SAFETYNET mission's SecGen/VM integration. Run the `.claude/skills/mission-secgen-review/SKILL.md` skill in full — it is the method. This brief adds the facts already established so you don't re-derive them, and the scope limits for running in parallel with other missions' agents. ([cliffe/BreakEscape](https://github.com/cliffe/BreakEscape/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/SECGEN_REVIEW_BRIEF.md) · ★6)

- **second-opinion**: Adversarial, fresh-context review of the current working-tree diff before wrapping up or committing. ([staffanm/ferenda](https://github.com/staffanm/ferenda/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/second-opinion.md) · ★42)
- **second-opinion**: Читающий-только советчик на самой сильной модели. Получает решение (или диф) и названные ограничения, возвращает вердикт: отдавать / сначала починить вот это / передумать. Советует, но никогда не правит. - **second-opinion**: 独立したセカンドオピニオンを提供する読み取り専用エージェント。 問題定義・前提・設計案を受け取り、cold-read の構造化された独立見解を返す。 second-opinion スキルのルートA（既定）として、gstack office-hours Phase 3.5 や 汎用的なセカンドオピニオンで使用。codex CLI 検出時に選べるルートB（外部 cold-read）は このエージェントを介さず SKILL.md が直接 `codex exec` を呼ぶ。 使用例: - user: "このアイデアにセカンドオピニオンがほしい" assistant: "second-opinion エージェントで独立した見解を取得します。" 使わない場面: - 会話の文脈を踏まえた技術相談は senpai / senior-engineer に振る。 このエージェントは会話履歴を持たず、渡されたコンテキストだけで判断する cold-read 専用。 - 実装の是非を指摘してほしい場合は tacchi（実態突合・過剰約束の検出）に振る。 ([todoroki-godai/evolve-anything](https://github.com/todoroki-godai/evolve-anything/blob/HEAD/agents/second-opinion.md) · ★1)
- **second-opinion**: Invoke only after the `stuck-recovery` skill has already been loaded and at least one deliberate strategy switch has also failed, or after three or more distinct hypotheses have been tested against the same symptom without resolution. - **second-opinion**: Fresh-context UI reviewer for Via. Sees only screenshots and a one-line brief, never the code. Use only from /via audit and /via check, when via_second_opinion.py exits 10. - **second-order**: Read-only consequence auditor.
